# Generated assets ledger

Budget set by the designer: $80, doubled from $40 on 2026-10-14 (the provider cap was $50 and must be raised by the designer in the OpenAI billing settings before spend passes it); the first art pass (docs/art/BRIEF.md) was capped at $15, raised by the designer to $25 on 2026-10-03 for the Gravity Golf exotic medals and Checkpoint Pack 3, and by another $15 to $40 the same day. Generation stops when the total below reaches it. The table covers the game library only. The OpenAI account was also used for 35 non-game edits of two venue photos for the designer's wedding (2026-10-11 to 2026-10-14), $4.82 in total, not listed per image and kept out of the repo because the photos are not ours; add it to the table total for the account's true spend ($39.85 on 2026-10-14). Costs are estimated from the API usage block at $40 per million output image tokens (the gpt-image-1 rate, an upper bound for gpt-image-1.5).

The skin must read as frost, clearly different from the plain gunmetal original even at thumbnail size Transparent background: the subject alone, isolated, no scene, no backdrop, no floor, no shadow on the ground. PNG, 1024x1024. [reference image: ref-make-my-day.png] |
